if(equals(coalesce(items('Apply_to_each_SelectedDocs')?['Progress']?['Value'],items('Apply_to_each_SelectedDocs')?['Status']),'Not started'),'#334155',\n              '#3730A3'\n            )\n          )\n        )\n      };\">\n      @{\n        toUpper(\n          coalesce(\n            items('Apply_to_each_SelectedDocs')?['Progress']?['Value'],\n            items('Apply_to_each_SelectedDocs')?['Status'],\n            outputs('Get_item_Task')?['body/Progress']?['Value'],\n            'Created'\n          )\n        )\n      }\n    </span>\n  </td>\n\n  <!-- Link -->\n  <td style=\"padding:12px;border-bottom:1px solid #E6ECF2;\">\n    <a href=\"@{outputs('Get_item_Doc')?['body/AttachmentLink']}\"\n    style=\"display:inline-block;\n           background:#05583B;\n           color:#FFFFFF;\n           font-family:Verdana,Arial,sans-serif;\n           font-size:11px;\n           font-weight:700;\n           padding:8px 12px;\n           border-radius:10px;\n           text-decoration:none;\">\n      Open\n    </a>\n  </td>\n\n</tr>\n"

```json
  "value": "@concat(\n  'https://taskack.page.gd/acknowledge.html',\n  '?items=',\n  if(\n    greater(length(coalesce(triggerBody()?['SelectedItems'], json('[]'))), 0),\n    join(\n      select(coalesce(triggerBody()?['SelectedItems'], json('[]')), string(item()?['ID'])),\n      ','\n    ),\n    string(coalesce(outputs('Get_item_Task')?['body/ID'], 0))\n  ),\n  '&name=',\n  uriComponent(\n    first(\n      split(\n        trim(replace(replace(string(coalesce(outputs('Get_item_Task')?['body/AssignedTo'], '')), '\\n', ' '), '\\r', ' ')),\n        '@'\n      )\n    )\n  ),\n  '&email=',\n  uriComponent(\n    trim(replace(replace(string(coalesce(outputs('Get_item_Task')?['body/AssignedTo'], '')), '\\n', ' '), '\\r', ' '))\n  ),\n  '&title=',\n  uriComponent(\n    trim(replace(replace(string(coalesce(outputs('Get_item_Task')?['body/Title'], 'Bulk Assignment')), '\\n', ' '), '\\r', ' '))\n  ),\n  '&ref=',\n  uriComponent(\n    if(\n      greater(length(coalesce(triggerBody()?['SelectedItems'], json('[]'))), 0),\n      concat('BULK-', variables('varBatchBulkId')),\n      concat('TASK-', string(coalesce(outputs('Get_item_Task')?['body/ID'], 0)))\n    )\n  ),\n  '&batch=',\n  uriComponent(variables('varBatchBulkId'))\n)"
}
```

```json
"\n\n\n  <meta>\n  <meta>\n  <title>NITDA • Bulk Assignment_cOMPOSE3_2</title>\n  <style>\n    /* Email-friendly (no external assets required) */\n    :root{\n      --nitda-deep-green:#05583B;\n      --nitda-smart-green:#17B255;\n      --nitda-black:#373435;\n      --ink:#0b1b14;\n      --muted:#48685c;\n      --bg:#eef7f2;\n      --card:#ffffff;\n      --border:rgba(5,88,59,.16);\n    }\n    body{margin:0;padding:0;background:var(--bg);font-family:Verdana,Arial,sans-serif;color:var(--ink);}\n    .wrap{width:100%;background:var(--bg);padding:24px 12px;}\n    .shell{max-width:980px;margin:0 auto;background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:18px;overflow:hidden;}\n    .topbar{background:var(--nitda-deep-green);color:#fff;padding:14px 16px;position:relative;}\n    .topbar:after{content:\"\";display:block;height:4px;background:var(--nitda-smart-green);position:absolute;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;}\n    .brand{font-size:13px;font-weight:700;line-height:1.2;margin:0;}\n    .subbrand{font-size:11px;opacity:.92;margin:4px 0 0;line-height:1.2;}\n    .pill{display:inline-block;margin:8px 8px 0 0;padding:7px 10px;border-radius:999px;\n          background:rgba(255,255,255,.10);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.18);\n          font-size:11px;font-weight:700;white-space:nowrap;}\n    .content{padding:16px;}\n    .hero{border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:16px;background:#f6fbf8;padding:14px 14px;}\n    .kicker{font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700;margin:0 0 8px;}\n    .title{font-size:18px;font-weight:700;color:var(--nitda-black);margin:0;line-height:1.25;}\n    .subtitle{font-size:12px;color:#1f3a30;line-height:1.55;margin:10px 0 0;}\n    .grid{margin-top:14px;}\n    .card{border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:16px;overflow:hidden;background:#fff;}\n    .card-hd{background:#f6fbf8;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);padding:12px 14px;}\n    .card-hd h3{margin:0;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--nitda-black);}\n    .card-bd{padding:12px 14px;}\n    table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;}\n    th{background:#eef7f2;text-align:left;font-size:11px;color:#1f3a30;padding:10px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase;}\n    td{vertical-align:top;}\n    .kv{width:100%;border-collapse:separate;border-spacing:0 10px;}\n    .kv td{padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:14px;background:#f6fbf8;}\n    .k{font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.10em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700;margin:0 0 6px;}\n    .v{font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--nitda-black);margin:0;word-break:break-word;}\n    .mono{font-family:Consolas,monospace;font-weight:700;}\n    .footer{padding:12px 14px;border-top:1px solid var(--border);background:#ffffff;}\n    .stamp{display:inline-block;margin:6px 8px 0 0;padding:6px 10px;border-radius:999px;\n           border:1px dashed rgba(5,88,59,.30);background:#f6fbf8;font-family:Consolas,monospace;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#1f3a30;}\n  </style>\n\n\n\n  <div class=\"wrap\">\n    <div class=\"shell\">\n\n      <!-- @{toUpper(string(coalesce(triggerBody()?['NewActivityTask']?['Priority'], 'Normal')))}\n        </span>\n      </div>\n\n      <div class=\"content\">\n\n        <!-- HERO -->\n        <div class=\"card-hd\">\n              <h3>Tasks (@{if(empty(variables('varTasksArray')), 0, length(variables('varTasksArray')))})</h3>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"card-bd\"                   <p class=\"v\">@{string(coalesce(triggerBody()?['NewActivityTask']?['CopyTo'], '—'))}</p>\n                  </td>\n                </tr>\n\n                <tr>\n                  <td>\n                    <p class=\"k\">Primary DSU</p>\n                    <p class=\"v\">@{string(coalesce(triggerBody()?['NewActivityTask']?['PrimaryDSU'], '—'))}</p>\n                  </td>\n                  <td>\n                    <p class=\"k\">Support DSU</p>\n                    <p class=\"v\">@{string(coalesce(triggerBody()?['NewActivityTask']?['SupportDSU'], '—'))}</p>\n .brand{font-size:13px;font-weight:700;line-height:1.2;margin:0;}\n    .subbrand{font-size:11px;opacity:.92;margin:4px 0 0;line-height:1.2;}\n    .pill{display:inline-block;margin:8px 8px 0 .content{padding:16px;}\n    .hero{border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:16px;background:#f6fbf8;padding:14px 14px;}\n    .kicker{font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700;margin:0 0 8px;}\n    .title{font-size:18px;font-weight:700;color:var(--nitda-black);margin:0;line-height:1.25;}\n    .subtitle{font-size:12px;color:#1f3a30;line-height:1.55;margin:10px 0 0;}\n    .grid{margin-top:14px;}\n    .card{border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:16px;overflow:hidden;background:#fff;}\n    .card-hd{background:#f6fbf8;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);padding:12px 14px;}\n    .card-hd h3{margin:0;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--nitda-black);}\n    .card-bd{padding:12px 14px;}\n    table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;}\n    th{background:#eef7f2;text-align:left;font-size:11px;color:#1f3a30;padding:10px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase;}\n    td{vertical-align:top;}\n    .kv{width:100%;border-collapse:separate;border-spacing:0 10px;}\n    .kv td{padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:14px;background:#f6fbf8;}\n    .k{font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.10em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--muted);font-weight:700;margin:0 0 6px;}\n    .v{font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--nitda-black);margin:0;word-break:break-word;}\n    .mono{font-family:Consolas,monospace;font-weight:700;}\n    .footer{padding:12px 14px;border-top:1px solid var(--border);background:#ffffff;}\n    .stamp{display:inline-block;margin:6px 8px 0 0;padding:6px 10px;border-radius:999px;\n           border:1px dashed rgba(5,88,59,.30);background:#f6fbf8;font-family:Consolas,monospace;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#1f3a30;}\n  </style>\n\n\n\n  <div class=\"wrap\">\n    <div class=\"shell\">\n\n      <!-- HEADER -->\n      <div class=\"topbar\">\n        <p class=\"brand\">Assignment Workflow</p>\n        <p class=\"subbrand\">National Information Technology Development Agency</p>\n\n        <span class=\"pill\">\n          MODE:\n          @{toUpper(string(coalesce(triggerBody()?['mode'], 'bulkassign')))}\n        </span>\n\n        <span class=\"pill\">\n          PRIORITY:\n class=\"hero\">\n          <p class=\"kicker\">\n            @{string(coalesce(triggerBody()?['NewActivityTask']?['Category'], 'N/A'))}\n            •\n            @{string(coalesce(triggerBody()?['NewActivityTask']?['SubCategory'], 'N/A'))}\n          </p>\n\n          <h2 class=\"title\">\n            @{string(coalesce(triggerBody()?['NewActivityTask']?['Title'], 'Bulk Assignment Summary'))}\n          </h2>\n\n          <p class=\"subtitle\">\n            The following\n            <strong>@{if(empty(variables('varAllTasksArray')), 0, length(variables('varAllTasksArray')))}</strong>\n            item(s) were created/updated in the workflow. Review the task list and confirm assignee details.\n          </p>\n        </div>\n\n        <!-- TASKS TABLE -->\n        <div class=\"grid\">\n          <div class=\"card\">\n            <div class=\"card-hd\">\n              <h3>Tasks (@{if(empty(variables('varTasksArray')), 0, length(variables('varTasksArray')))})</h3>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"card-bd\" style=\"padding:0;\">\n              \n                  @{string(coalesce(variables('varTaskRowsHtml'), ''))}\n                <table>\n                <thead>\n                  <tr>\n                    <th style=\"width:46%;\">Document / Task</th>\n                    <th style=\"width:16%;\">Ref ID</th>\n                    <th style=\"width:18%;\">Status</th>\n                    <th style=\"width:20%;\">Link</th>\n                  </tr>\n                </thead>\n\n                <tbody></tbody>\n              </table>\n\n              <!-- Empty-state (renders only when no rows) -->\n              @{if(\n                equals(if(empty(variables('varTasksArray')), 0, length(variables('varTasksArray'))), 0),\n                '<div style=\"padding:14px;font-size:12px;color:#48685c;\">No tasks were generated.</div>',\n                ''\n              )}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        <!-- DETAILS -->\n        <div class=\"grid\" style=\"margin-top:14px;\">\n          <div class=\"card\">\n            <div class=\"card-hd\">\n              <h3>Assignment Details</h3>\n            </div>\n            <div class=\"card-bd\">\n\n              <table class=\"kv\">\n                <tbody><tr>\n                  <td>\n                    <p class=\"k\">Assigned To</p>\n                    <p class=\"v\">@{string(coalesce(triggerBody()?['NewActivityTask']?['AssignedTo'], '—'))}</p>\n                  </td>\n                  <td>\n                    <p class=\"k\">Copy To</p>\n                    <p class=\"v\">@{string(coalesce(triggerBody()?['NewActivityTask']?['CopyTo'], '—'))}</p>\n                  </td>\n                </tr>\n\n                <tr>\n                  <td>\n                    <p class=\"k\">Primary DSU</p>\n                    <p class=\"v\">@{string(coalesce(triggerBody()?['NewActivityTask']?['PrimaryDSU'], '—'))}</p>\n                  </td>\n                  <td>\n                    <p class=\"k\">Support DSU</p>\n                    <p class=\"v\">@{string(coalesce(triggerBody()?['NewActivityTask']?['SupportDSU'], '—'))}</p>\n                  </td>\n                </tr>\n\n                <tr>\n                  <td>\n                    <p class=\"k\">Timeline Days</p>\n                    <p class=\"v mono\">@{string(coalesce(triggerBody()?['NewActivityTask']?['TimelineDays'], '—'))}</p>\n                  </td>\n                  <td>\n                    <p class=\"k\">Due Date</p>\n          formatDateTime(string(triggerBody()?['NewActivityTask']?['DueDate']), 'dd-MMM-yyyy')\n                      )}\n                    </p>\n                  </td>\n                </tr>\n\n                <tr>\n                  <td>\n                    <p class=\"k\">Task Type</p>\n                    <p class=\"v\">@{string(coalesce(triggerBody()?['NewActivityTask']?['TaskType'], 'Standard Task'))}</p>\n                  </td>\n                  <td>\n                    <p class=\"k\">Grouping</p>\n                    <p class=\"v\">@{string(coalesce(triggerBody()?['NewActivityTask']?['GTQGrouping'], 'GTQGrouping'))}</p>\n                  </td>\n                </tr>\n              </tbody></table>\n\n              <div style=\"margin-top:12px;padding:12px;border-radius:14px;border:1px solid rgba(5,88,59,.16);background:#f6fbf8;\">\n                <p class=\"k\" style=\"margin:0 0 8px;\">Comments</p>\n                <p class=\"v\" style=\"font-weight:600;color:#48685c;margin:0;\">\n                  @{string(coalesce(triggerBody()?['NewActivityTask']?['Comments'], 'No comments provided.'))}\n                </p>\n              </div>\n\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n      </div>\n\n      <!-- FOOTER -->\n      <div class=\"footer\">\n        <span class=\"stamp\">assignmentType:@{string(coalesce(triggerBody()?['NewActivityTask']?['AssignmentType'], 'newassignment'))}</span>\n        <span class=\"stamp\">tasks:@{if(empty(variables('varTasksArray')), 0, length(variables('varTasksArray')))}</span>\n        <span class=\"stamp\">run:@{formatDateTime(utcNow(),'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm')} UTC</span>\n      </div>\n\n    </div>\n  </div>\n\n\n"
```

```json
{
  "auditVersion": "1.0",
  "generatedAtUtc": "@{utcNow()}",
  "requestId": "@{coalesce(triggerBody()?['requestId'], guid())}",
  "mode": "@{if(greater(length(coalesce(triggerBody()?['SelectedItems'], json('[]'))), 0), 'bulk', if(not(empty(triggerBody()?['Selected'])), 'single', 'unknown'))}",
  "counts": {
    "selectedItemsCount": "@{length(coalesce(triggerBody()?['SelectedItems'], json('[]')))}",
    "hasSelectedSingle": "@{if(empty(triggerBody()?['Selected']), false, true)}",
    "tasksCreated": "@{coalesce(variables('varTasksCreated'), 0)}",
    "docsUpdated": "@{coalesce(variables('varDocsUpdated'), 0)}",
    "notificationsSent": "@{coalesce(variables('varNotificationsSent'), 0)}",
    "failed": "@{coalesce(variables('varFailed'), 0)}",
    "taskDocPairsCount": "@{length(coalesce(variables('varTasksArray'), json('[]')))}",
    "allDocsArrayCount": "@{length(coalesce(variables('varAllDocsArray'), json('[]')))}",
    "allTasksArrayCount": "@{length(coalesce(variables('varAllTasksArray'), json('[]')))}"
  },
  "trigger": "@{coalesce(triggerBody(), json('{}'))}",
  "triggerOutputs": "@{coalesce(triggerOutputs(), json('{}'))}",
  "variables": {
    "varAllDocsArray": "@{coalesce(variables('varAllDocsArray'), json('[]'))}",
    "varAllTasksArray": "@{coalesce(variables('varAllTasksArray'), json('[]'))}",
    "varTasksArray": "@{coalesce(variables('varTasksArray'), json('[]'))}",
    "varResults": "@{coalesce(variables('varResults'), json('[]'))}",
    "varErrors": "@{coalesce(variables('varErrors'), json('[]'))}",
    "rowHtml": "@{coalesce(variables('rowHtml'), '')}",
    "varStatusCode": "@{coalesce(variables('varStatusCode'), 0)}",
    "varStartTicks": "@{coalesce(variables('varStartTicks'), 0)}"
  },
  "snapshot": {
    "firstDoc": "@{if(greater(length(coalesce(variables('varAllDocsArray'), json('[]'))), 0), first(variables('varAllDocsArray')), json('{}'))}",
    "firstTask": "@{if(greater(length(coalesce(variables('varAllTasksArray'), json('[]'))), 0), first(variables('varAllTasksArray')), json('{}'))}",
    "firstPair": "@{if(greater(length(coalesce(variables('varTasksArray'), json('[]'))), 0), first(variables('varTasksArray')), json('{}'))}"
  }
}
```

|---|---|

### `Send_an_email_(V2)_r1_v2`

| Field | As provisioned |
|---|---|
| Type | `OpenApiConnection` |
| Kind | — |
| Description | `Updated Attachments parameter to use an array of objects with base64-encoded content, as required by the Send an email (V2) action.` |
| Runs after | `Send_an_email_(V2)_r1_v1` (Succeeded) |
| Connector | `shared_office365-2` |
| Operation | `SendEmailV2` |
